Mesa 11.2.2 source component notices
===================================

Source archive: mesa-11.2.2.tar.xz
SHA-256: 40e148812388ec7c6d7b6657d5a16e2e8dabba8b97ddfceea5197947647bdfb4

The following copyright and license comment blocks are reproduced
from source components used by the Windows software-renderer build paths.
Repeated identical blocks are grouped by source path. The collection covers
Mesa core, GLSL/compiler, GL dispatch, utility and Gallium software-renderer
components and their headers. It is a conservative notice collection, not
an assertion that every listed file was linked into the shipped DLL.
The exact vendor build configuration remains subject to the source record.

Bison-generated parsers retain their special skeleton exception below; those
notices do not label the entire Mesa runtime as GPL. See Mesa-11.2.2-LICENSE.txt
for the upstream component-level license explanation.
